# About this item

*    HEAVY-DUTY DAMAGED-SCREW EXTRACTOR SET: Tackle frozen, broken, stripped, and damaged screws and bolts with this set of screw extractors. These extractors will help you remove even the toughest broken fasteners with speed and efficiency. 
*    DURABLE BOLT-EXTRACTOR SET: Each industrial-grade extractor is constructed from premium chrome molybdenum steel for superior toughness. The aggressive multiplane teeth feature a left-hand spiral design to provide extra gripping power and faster threading. 
*    EASY-OUT BOLT-EXTRACTOR SET: This damaged-screw extractor set contains 25 pieces that feature precision-engineered and precision-machined hexagon heads for applying maximum torque when paired with wrenches or sockets. 
*    THE RIGHT SIZE: Always have the right extractor bit when it’s time to deal with broken or frozen screws. This set has extractors from 1/8 inch (3.5 mm) to 7/8 inch (23 mm) in 1/32-inch increments. Each piece has a laser-etched marking for easy identification. 
*    VERSATILE: Our extractor set comes in a durable and convenient storage case for easy storage and travel. It’s ideal for use at home, in the garage, at the construction site, or in your shop. 

›[See more product details](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B01KN9YVWA#productDetails)

Customer Review Review of the NEIKO 10038A Left-Hand Drill-Bit Set[See Full Review](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B01KN9YVWA#)

## Product Description

The NEIKO 04204A Hex-Head Multispline Screw and Bolt Extractor Set is a must-have tool set for mechanics, carpenters, DIY enthusiasts, and professionals alike. This hex-bolt remover set can easily remove stubborn, stripped, frozen, or broken screws and bolts at home or work. Each precisely-machined hex head is constructed from tough, resilient chrome molybdenum steel for superior performance under high torque. Each piece features aggressive multiplane teeth with a left-hand spiral design for extra gripping power and faster threading.  
  
This extractor set includes 25 extractors in a wide variety of sizes: 1/8" (M3.5), 5/32" (M4), 3/16" (M5), 7/32" (M5.5), 1/4" (M6.5), 9/32" (M7.5), 5/16" (M8), 11/32" (M9), 3/8" (M10), 13/32" (M11), 7/16" (M11.5), 15/32" (M12), 1/2" (M13), 17/32" (M14), 9/16" (M14.5), 19/32" (M15.5), 5/8" (M16), 21/32" (M17), 11/16" (M18), 23/32" (M18.5), 3/4" (M19.5), 25/32" (M20), 13/16" (M21), 27/32" (M22), and 7/8" (M23).  
  
This set comes in a durable and convenient plastic case for easy organization and travel. Take it with you to the jobsite, the garage, or the construction site, or keep a set at home for easy repairs. When it comes to tools that help you fix what’s broken, NEIKO has your back.

[How customer reviews and ratings work](javascript:void(0))

Customer Reviews, including Product Star Ratings help customers to learn more about the product and decide whether it is the right product for them.

To calculate the overall star rating and percentage breakdown by star, we don’t use a simple average. Instead, our system considers things like how recent a review is and if the reviewer bought the item on Amazon. It also analyzed reviews to verify trustworthiness.

### Customers say

Customers find the bolt extractor convenient, describing it as a life saver. The tool's appearance receives positive feedback, and customers appreciate its variety of sizes. However, durability and sharpness receive mixed reviews - while some say it stands up to abuse well and has sharp serrations, others report pieces breaking off too easily and not being sharp enough. Value for money is also mixed, with some finding it worth the price while others say you get what you pay for.

# Product Summary: NEIKO 04204A Hex-Head Multispline Screw and Bolt Extractor Set, Easy-Out Screw Extraction, Broken Bolt Remover, Stripped Fastener Tool, 1/8 Inch to 7/8 Inch in 1/32-Inch Increments, CrMo, 25 Pieces

From NEIKO

4.4 out of 5 stars, 1,300 ratings

## About this Item

*   HEAVY-DUTY DAMAGED-SCREW EXTRACTOR SET: Tackle frozen, broken, stripped, and damaged screws and bolts with this set of screw extractors. These extractors will help you remove even the toughest broken fasteners with speed and efficiency.
*   DURABLE BOLT-EXTRACTOR SET: Each industrial-grade extractor is constructed from premium chrome molybdenum steel for superior toughness. The aggressive multiplane teeth feature a left-hand spiral design to provide extra gripping power and faster threading.
*   EASY-OUT BOLT-EXTRACTOR SET: This damaged-screw extractor set contains 25 pieces that feature precision-engineered and precision-machined hexagon heads for applying maximum torque when paired with wrenches or sockets.
*   THE RIGHT SIZE: Always have the right extractor bit when it’s time to deal with broken or frozen screws. This set has extractors from 1/8 inch (3.5 mm) to 7/8 inch (23 mm) in 1/32-inch increments. Each piece has a laser-etched marking for easy identification.
*   VERSATILE: Our extractor set comes in a durable and convenient storage case for easy storage and travel. It’s ideal for use at home, in the garage, at the construction site, or in your shop.

## Product Description

 The NEIKO 04204A Hex-Head Multispline Screw and Bolt Extractor Set is a must-have tool set for mechanics, carpenters, DIY enthusiasts, and professionals alike. This hex-bolt remover set can easily remove stubborn, stripped, frozen, or broken screws and bolts at home or work. Each precisely-machined hex head is constructed from tough, resilient chrome molybdenum steel for superior performance under high torque. Each piece features aggressive multiplane teeth with a left-hand spiral design for extra gripping power and faster threading. This extractor set includes 25 extractors in a wide variety of sizes: 1/8" (M3.5), 5/32" (M4), 3/16" (M5), 7/32" (M5.5), 1/4" (M6.5), 9/32" (M7.5), 5/16" (M8), 11/32" (M9), 3/8" (M10), 13/32" (M11), 7/16" (M11.5), 15/32" (M12), 1/2" (M13), 17/32" (M14), 9/16" (M14.5), 19/32" (M15.5), 5/8" (M16), 21/32" (M17), 11/16" (M18), 23/32" (M18.5), 3/4" (M19.5), 25/32" (M20), 13/16" (M21), 27/32" (M22), and 7/8" (M23). This set comes in a durable and convenient plastic case for easy organization and travel. Take it with you to the jobsite, the garage, or the construction site, or keep a set at home for easy repairs. When it comes to tools that help you fix what’s broken, NEIKO has your back.
